The paper also reports on novel benchmarks, including some that address aspects of performance that so far have not been available in benchmarks for European Portuguese, namely model safeguards and alignment to Portuguese culture. The leaderboard is available at https://huggingface.co/spaces/PORTULAN/portuguese-llm-leaderboard.

</details>


### [113] [Learning from Child-Directed Speech in Two-Language Scenarios: A French-English Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12906)
*Liel Binyamin,Elior Sulem*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究扩展了BabyBERTa至英法双语场景，系统比较了儿童导向语音与多领域语料在大小匹配条件下的语言模型表现，发现不同训练数据对语法与语义任务有情境依赖性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有发展型语言模型研究多集中于英语，缺乏对多语言环境的探讨，本文旨在填补这一空白，探索在严格控制数据规模条件下跨语言设置中的模型表现。

Method: 通过扩展BabyBERTa到英法双语场景，在单语、双语和跨语言设置下使用儿童导向语音（约250万token）和多领域语料（约1000万token）进行训练，并引入新的法语QAMR、QASRL及多领域语料资源以支持公平评估。

Result: 实验表明：维基百科数据持续提升语义任务表现，而儿童导向语音在单语环境下更利于语法判断；双语预训练显著提升文本蕴含任务表现，尤其对法语效果明显；该模式在BabyBERTa、RoBERTa和LTG-BERT中具有一致性。

Conclusion: 不同训练数据类型对语法与语义任务的影响具有情境依赖性，双语预训练可带来显著增益，且结果在多种架构中具有一致性，验证了发展型模型在多语言设置下的可行性。

Abstract: Research on developmentally plausible language models has largely focused on English, leaving open questions about multilingual settings. We present a systematic study of compact language models by extending BabyBERTa to English-French scenarios under strictly size-matched data conditions, covering monolingual, bilingual, and cross-lingual settings. Our design contrasts two types of training corpora: (i) child-directed speech (about 2.5M tokens), following BabyBERTa and related work, and (ii) multi-domain corpora (about 10M tokens), extending the BabyLM framework to French. To enable fair evaluation, we also introduce new resources, including French versions of QAMR and QASRL, as well as English and French multi-domain corpora.
  We evaluate the models on both syntactic and semantic tasks and compare them with models trained on Wikipedia-only data. The results reveal context-dependent effects: training on Wikipedia consistently benefits semantic tasks, whereas child-directed speech improves grammatical judgments in monolingual settings. Bilingual pretraining yields notable gains for textual entailment, with particularly strong improvements for French. Importantly, similar patterns emerge across BabyBERTa, RoBERTa, and LTG-BERT, suggesting consistent trends across architectures.

</details>


### [114] [DS$^2$-Instruct: Domain-Specific Data Synthesis for Large Language Models Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12932)
*Ruiyao Xu,Noelle I. Samia,Han Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需人工监督的零样本框架DS²-Instruct，用于生成特定领域的大规模指令数据集，显著提升了模型在多个专业领域的微调效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了降低创建高质量领域特定指令微调数据集的成本，并克服现有数据合成方法在专业术语和推理模式上的不足。

Method: 首先生成任务相关的关键词以确保领域覆盖全面，然后结合布鲁姆分类法中的不同认知层次配对生成多样化指令，最后通过自洽性验证保证数据质量。

Result: 在七个具有挑战性的领域（如数学、金融和逻辑推理）中生成了数据集，实验表明基于这些数据微调的模型显著优于现有数据生成方法。

Conclusion: DS²-Instruct能够有效生成高质量、多样化的领域特定指令数据，为大语言模型的领域适配提供了低成本且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Adapting Large Language Models (LLMs) to specialized domains requires high-quality instruction tuning datasets, which are expensive to create through human annotation. Existing data synthesis methods focus on general-purpose tasks and fail to capture domain-specific terminology and reasoning patterns. To address this, we introduce DS$^2$-Instruct, a zero-shot framework that generates domain-specific instruction datasets without human supervision. Our approach first generates task-informed keywords to ensure comprehensive domain coverage. It then creates diverse instructions by pairing these keywords with different cognitive levels from Bloom's Taxonomy. Finally, it uses self-consistency validation to ensure data quality. We apply this framework to generate datasets across seven challenging domains, such as mathematics, finance, and logical reasoning. Comprehensive evaluation demonstrates that models fine-tuned on our generated data achieve substantial improvements over existing data generation methods.

</details>


### [115] [Long-form RewardBench: Evaluating Reward Models for Long-form Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12963)
*Hui Huang,Yancheng He,Wei Liu,Muyun Yang,Jiaheng Liu,Kehai Chen,Bing Xu,Conghui Zhu,Hailong Cao,Tiejun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Long-form RewardBench，首个专门针对长文本生成的奖励模型评测基准，涵盖五个子任务，并通过多阶段数据收集和大量实验发现现有模型在长文本奖励建模方面能力不足，同时揭示了错误位置与响应长度对性能的影响，以及分类器模型相较于生成式模型具有更好的泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏针对长文本生成场景下的奖励模型评估方法，尽管该任务在实际应用中至关重要，因此需要一个专门的测试平台来推动这一领域的发展。

Method: 设计了一个名为Long-form RewardBench的基准，包含QA、RAG、Chat、Writing和Reasoning五个关键子任务；采用多阶段数据收集流程获取指令和偏好数据；对20多个主流奖励模型（包括分类器和生成式模型）进行了广泛实验；并提出了一种新的“长文本海中捞针”测试以分析模型性能与错误位置及响应长度的关系。

Result: 实验表明现有奖励模型在长文本生成任务中表现不佳；新提出的‘长文本海中捞针’测试揭示了性能与错误位置和响应长度之间的相关性，且分类器与生成式模型表现出不同特征；分类器在相同训练数据下展现出更强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: Long-form RewardBench填补了长文本奖励建模评估的空白，为该领域提供了可靠的进展可视化平台，并指出当前模型的局限性及未来改进方向。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of reinforcement learning-based alignment highlights the growing importance of reward models. Various benchmarks have been built to evaluate reward models in various domains and scenarios. However, a significant gap remains in assessing reward models for long-form generation, despite its critical role in real-world applications. To bridge this, we introduce Long-form RewardBench, the first reward modeling testbed specifically designed for long-form generation. Our benchmark encompasses five key subtasks: QA, RAG, Chat, Writing, and Reasoning. We collected instruction and preference data through a meticulously designed multi-stage data collection process, and conducted extensive experiments on 20+ mainstream reward models, including both classifiers and generative models. Our findings reveal that current models still lack long-form reward modeling capabilities. Furthermore, we designed a novel Long-form Needle-in-a-Haystack Test, which revealed a correlation between reward modeling performance and the error's position within a response, as well as the overall response length, with distinct characteristics observed between classification and generative models. Finally, we demonstrate that classifiers exhibit better generalizability compared to generative models trained on the same data. As the first benchmark for long-form reward modeling, this work aims to offer a robust platform for visualizing progress in this crucial area.

</details>


### [116] [Is Human Annotation Necessary? Iterative MBR Distillation for Error Span Detection in Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12983)
*Boxuan Lyu,Haiyue Song,Zhi Qu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于最小贝叶斯风险（MBR）解码的迭代蒸馏框架，用于无需人工标注的机器翻译错误跨度检测，实验表明该方法在系统级和跨度级上优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决机器翻译评估中错误跨度检测依赖昂贵且不一致的人工标注数据的问题，探索无需人工标注的自演化方法。

Method: 提出名为Iterative MBR Distillation for ESD的框架，利用现成的大语言模型生成伪标签，结合MBR解码实现自我进化训练，避免使用人工标注数据。

Result: 在WMT Metrics共享任务数据集上的实验显示，仅使用自生成伪标签训练的模型在系统级和跨度级性能上优于未调整的基模型和基于人工标注的监督基线，句子级表现也具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 所提出的自演化框架有效减少了对人工标注的依赖，并在多个评估层级上实现了优越的错误跨度检测性能。

Abstract: Error Span Detection (ESD) is a crucial subtask in Machine Translation (MT) evaluation, aiming to identify the location and severity of translation errors. While fine-tuning models on human-annotated data improves ESD performance, acquiring such data is expensive and prone to inconsistencies among annotators. To address this, we propose a novel self-evolution framework based on Minimum Bayes Risk (MBR) decoding, named Iterative MBR Distillation for ESD, which eliminates the reliance on human annotations by leveraging an off-the-shelf LLM to generate pseudo-labels.Extensive experiments on the WMT Metrics Shared Task datasets demonstrate that models trained solely on these self-generated pseudo-labels outperform both unadapted base model and supervised baselines trained on human annotations at the system and span levels, while maintaining competitive sentence-level performance.

</details>


### [117] [Interpretable Semantic Gradients in SSD: A PCA Sweep Approach and a Case Study on AI Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13038)
*Hubert Plisiecki,Maria Leniarska,Jan Piotrowski,Marcin Zajenkowski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于监督语义差异（SSD）分析的PCA扫掠方法，以系统化选择主成分数量，提升语义梯度的可解释性与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SSD方法在PCA降维时缺乏系统性的组件数选择标准，导致研究者自由度过高，影响结果可靠性。

Method: 在回归前应用PCA扫掠程序，结合表征能力、梯度可解释性和K值邻域稳定性作为联合准则来确定主成分数。

Result: 在关于人工智能的文本语料库中，发现与‘钦慕型自恋’相关的稳定且可解释的语义梯度，表现为对AI的乐观合作态度与不信任嘲讽态度的对立；而‘竞争型自恋’未见显著模式。高维PCA解则产生松散、结构弱的聚类。

Conclusion: PCA扫掠法有效减少了分析中的主观自由度，同时保持了SSD方法对语义意义的深层解释力，支持更透明、心理上更有意义的文本分析。

Abstract: Supervised Semantic Differential (SSD) is a mixed quantitative-interpretive method that models how text meaning varies with continuous individual-difference variables by estimating a semantic gradient in an embedding space and interpreting its poles through clustering and text retrieval. SSD applies PCA before regression, but currently no systematic method exists for choosing the number of retained components, introducing avoidable researcher degrees of freedom in the analysis pipeline. We propose a PCA sweep procedure that treats dimensionality selection as a joint criterion over representation capacity, gradient interpretability, and stability across nearby values of K. We illustrate the method on a corpus of short posts about artificial intelligence written by Prolific participants who also completed Admiration and Rivalry narcissism scales. The sweep yields a stable, interpretable Admiration-related gradient contrasting optimistic, collaborative framings of AI with distrustful and derisive discourse, while no robust alignment emerges for Rivalry. We also show that a counterfactual using a high-PCA dimension solution heuristic produces diffuse, weakly structured clusters instead, reinforcing the value of the sweep-based choice of K. The case study shows how the PCA sweep constrains researcher degrees of freedom while preserving SSD's interpretive aims, supporting transparent and psychologically meaningful analyses of connotative meaning.

</details>


### [118] [Mending the Holes: Mitigating Reward Hacking in Reinforcement Learning for Multilingual Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13045)
*Yifeng Liu,Siqi Ouyang,Yatish Hosmane Revanasiddappa,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为WALAR的强化学习训练方法，仅使用单语文本提升大语言模型在低资源语言翻译中的性能，同时保持其在高资源语言上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练方法依赖高质量平行语料，在低资源语言中难以获得；同时基于源语言的多语言质量估计模型存在缺陷，导致强化学习效果不佳。

Method: 提出WALAR方法，结合词对齐和语言对齐技术，利用单语数据通过改进的质量估计奖励进行强化学习训练，避免放大原有模型缺陷。

Result: 在支持101种语言的LLM上持续训练，实验表明新模型在Flores-101数据集的1400个翻译方向上显著优于当前最强开源多语言LLM之一LLaMAX。

Conclusion: WALAR能有效提升大语言模型在大规模低资源语言翻译中的能力，且不损害高资源语言性能，为低资源语言翻译提供了可行的新路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capability in machine translation on high-resource language pairs, yet their performance on low-resource translation still lags behind. Existing post-training methods rely heavily on high-quality parallel data, which are often scarce or unavailable for low-resource languages. In this paper, we introduce WALAR, a reinforcement training method using only monolingual text to elevate LLMs' translation capabilities on massive low-resource languages while retaining their performance on high-resource languages. Our key insight is based on the observation of failure modes (or "holes") in existing source-based multilingual quality estimation (QE) models. Reinforcement learning (RL) using these QE models tends to amplify such holes, resulting in poorer multilingual LLMs. We develop techniques including word alignment and language alignment to mitigate such holes in WALAR's reward for RL training. We continually trained an LLM supporting translation of 101 languages using WALAR. The experiments show that our new model outperforms LLaMAX, one of the strongest open-source multilingual LLMs by a large margin on 1400 language directions on Flores-101 dataset.

</details>


### [119] [ESG-Bench: Benchmarking Long-Context ESG Reports for Hallucination Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13154)
*Siqi Sun,Ben Peng Wu,Mali Jin,Peizhen Bai,Hanpei Zhang,Xingyi Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了ESG-Bench，一个用于评估大型语言模型在理解ESG报告和减少幻觉方面表现的基准数据集，并通过链式思维提示和微调方法有效降低模型幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于ESG报告内容冗长复杂，难以自动化分析，且存在模型生成幻觉的问题，因此需要一个可靠的基准来支持可验证的、可扩展的ESG信息披露分析。

Method: 构建了一个基于真实ESG报告的人工标注问答数据集ESG-Bench，引入细粒度标签判断模型输出是否为事实支持或幻觉，并设计任务特定的链式思维（CoT）提示策略，对多个先进大模型进行微调。

Result: 实验表明，基于CoT的方法在减少幻觉方面显著优于标准提示和直接微调方法，且该提升效果可迁移到其他非ESG领域的问答基准。

Conclusion: ESG-Bench为在合规敏感场景下评估和改进大模型的可信推理提供了有效工具，证明了结合可验证性约束与CoT策略在缓解社会敏感领域中模型幻觉的有效性。

Abstract: As corporate responsibility increasingly incorporates environmental, social, and governance (ESG) criteria, ESG reporting is becoming a legal requirement in many regions and a key channel for documenting sustainability practices and assessing firms' long-term and ethical performance. However, the length and complexity of ESG disclosures make them difficult to interpret and automate the analysis reliably. To support scalable and trustworthy analysis, this paper introduces ESG-Bench, a benchmark dataset for ESG report understanding and hallucination mitigation in large language models (LLMs). ESG-Bench contains human-annotated question-answer (QA) pairs grounded in real-world ESG report contexts, with fine-grained labels indicating whether model outputs are factually supported or hallucinated. Framing ESG report analysis as a QA task with verifiability constraints enables systematic evaluation of LLMs' ability to extract and reason over ESG content and provides a new use case: mitigating hallucinations in socially sensitive, compliance-critical settings. We design task-specific Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting strategies and fine-tune multiple state-of-the-art LLMs on ESG-Bench using CoT-annotated rationales. Our experiments show that these CoT-based methods substantially outperform standard prompting and direct fine-tuning in reducing hallucinations, and that the gains transfer to existing QA benchmarks beyond the ESG domain.

</details>


### [120] [Neuron-Aware Data Selection In Instruction Tuning For Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13201)
*Xin Chen,Junchao Wu,Shu Yang,Runzhe Zhan,Zeyu Wu,Min Yang,Shujian Huang,Lidia S. Chao,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为NAIT的新框架，通过分析神经元激活模式的相似性来评估和选择对大语言模型（LLM）性能提升最有效的指令调优（IT）数据子集。实验表明，使用NAIT选出的10% Alpaca-GPT4数据子集在多个任务上均优于依赖外部高级模型或基于不确定性的方法，并揭示了神经元激活特征在不同能力间的可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过度的指令调优数据会降低大语言模型的性能，而高质量的小规模数据子集却能显著提升其能力。因此，如何从大量IT数据中识别出最高效的子集以发展特定或通用能力成为关键挑战。

Method: NAIT通过分析候选样本与目标能力期望激活特征之间的神经元激活模式相似性来进行评估和选样。它从目标领域的能力的域内数据集中捕捉神经元激活模式，构建可重用和可迁移的神经元激活特征。

Result: 在多个任务上，基于NAIT选出的10% Alpaca-GPT4子集进行训练始终优于依赖外部先进模型或基于不确定性的方法；发现具有逻辑推理和程序化特征的IT数据具备强泛化迁移能力，且存在一个稳定的核数据子集可普遍提升模型在多样化任务上的表现。

Conclusion: NAIT是一种高效且新颖的框架，能够通过神经元激活模式相似性有效筛选最优IT数据子集，不仅提升了LLM在特定和通用能力上的表现，还揭示了神经元激活特征在不同任务间的可迁移性，为高效指令调优提供了新思路。

Abstract: Instruction Tuning (IT) has been proven to be an effective approach to unlock the powerful capabilities of large language models (LLMs). Recent studies indicate that excessive IT data can degrade LLMs performance, while carefully selecting a small subset of high-quality IT data can significantly enhance their capabilities. Therefore, identifying the most efficient subset data from the IT dataset to effectively develop either specific or general abilities in LLMs has become a critical challenge. To address this, we propose a novel and efficient framework called NAIT. NAIT evaluates the impact of IT data on LLMs performance by analyzing the similarity of neuron activation patterns between the IT dataset and the target domain capability. Specifically, NAIT captures neuron activation patterns from in-domain datasets of target domain capabilities to construct reusable and transferable neuron activation features. It then evaluates and selects optimal samples based on the similarity between candidate samples and the expected activation features of the target capabilities. Experimental results show that training on the 10\% Alpaca-GPT4 IT data subset selected by NAIT consistently outperforms methods that rely on external advanced models or uncertainty-based features across various tasks. Our findings also reveal the transferability of neuron activation features across different capabilities of LLMs. In particular, IT data with more logical reasoning and programmatic features possesses strong general transferability, enabling models to develop stronger capabilities across multiple tasks, while a stable core subset of data is sufficient to consistently activate fundamental model capabilities and universally improve performance across diverse tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [Context-Enriched Natural Language Descriptions of Vessel Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12287)
*Kostas Patroumpas,Alexandros Troupiotis-Kapeliaris,Giannis Spiliopoulos,Panagiotis Betchavas,Dimitrios Skoutas,Dimitris Zissis,Nikos Bikakis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种上下文感知的轨迹抽象框架，将原始AIS轨迹数据转化为结构化、语义丰富的表示形式，并支持利用多源上下文信息和大语言模型生成受控自然语言描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始AIS轨迹数据噪声多、语义稀疏，难以直接用于人类理解或机器推理，需要一种能够提取高阶语义表示的方法。

Method: 设计一个上下文感知的轨迹抽象框架，对AIS轨迹进行分段，清洗并标注移动模式，结合地理、航海和气象等多源上下文信息，生成结构化且语义增强的轨迹片段，并利用大语言模型生成自然语言描述。

Result: 实验证明该框架能有效提升轨迹数据的语义密度、降低时空复杂性，并能利用多个大语言模型生成高质量的自然语言描述。

Conclusion: 所提出的抽象框架有助于下游分析任务，并推动AIS数据与大语言模型的融合，支持高层次的海事推理应用。

Abstract: We address the problem of transforming raw vessel trajectory data collected from AIS into structured and semantically enriched representations interpretable by humans and directly usable by machine reasoning systems. We propose a context-aware trajectory abstraction framework that segments noisy AIS sequences into distinct trips each consisting of clean, mobility-annotated episodes. Each episode is further enriched with multi-source contextual information, such as nearby geographic entities, offshore navigation features, and weather conditions. Crucially, such representations can support generation of controlled natural language descriptions using LLMs. We empirically examine the quality of such descriptions generated using several LLMs over AIS data along with open contextual features. By increasing semantic density and reducing spatiotemporal complexity, this abstraction can facilitate downstream analytics and enable integration with LLMs for higher-level maritime reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [122] [Efficient Reasoning with Balanced Thinking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12372)
*Yulin Li,Tengyao Tu,Li Ding,Junjie Wang,Huiling Zhen,Yixin Chen,Yong Li,Zhuotao Tian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReBalance是一种无需训练的框架，通过利用置信度动态调节大推理模型的推理过程，有效平衡过思考与欠思考问题，提升推理效率与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型在简单问题上容易出现过思考（冗余计算）或复杂问题上出现欠思考（探索不足），导致效率低下和准确性下降，现有方法难以兼顾两者，因此需要一种更灵活、通用且无需训练的解决方案。

Method: ReBalance使用置信度作为推理动态的连续指标，识别过思考（高置信方差）和欠思考（持续过度自信）；通过小规模数据集聚合隐藏状态构建推理模式原型，生成引导向量，并设计动态控制函数实时调节该向量的强度与方向，以剪枝冗余或促进探索。

Result: 在四个不同规模（0.5B至32B）的模型及九个数学、问答和编程任务基准上的实验表明，ReBalance显著减少了输出冗余，同时提高了准确率。

Conclusion: ReBalance提供了一种通用、无需训练、即插即用的方法，实现高效且稳健的大推理模型部署，有效平衡过思考与欠思考问题。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) have shown remarkable reasoning capabilities, yet they often suffer from overthinking, expending redundant computational steps on simple problems, or underthinking, failing to explore sufficient reasoning paths despite inherent capabilities. These issues lead to inefficiencies and potential inaccuracies, limiting practical deployment in resource-constrained settings. Existing methods to mitigate overthinking, such as suppressing reflective keywords or adjusting reasoning length, may inadvertently induce underthinking, compromising accuracy. Therefore, we propose ReBalance, a training-free framework that achieves efficient reasoning with balanced thinking. ReBalance leverages confidence as a continuous indicator of reasoning dynamics, identifying overthinking through high confidence variance and underthinking via consistent overconfidence. By aggregating hidden states from a small-scale dataset into reasoning mode prototypes, we compute a steering vector to guide LRMs' reasoning trajectories. A dynamic control function modulates this vector's strength and direction based on real-time confidence, pruning redundancy during overthinking, and promoting exploration during underthinking. Extensive experiments conducted on four models ranging from 0.5B to 32B, and across nine benchmarks in math reasoning, general question answering, and coding tasks demonstrate that ReBalance effectively reduces output redundancy while improving accuracy, offering a general, training-free, and plug-and-play strategy for efficient and robust LRM deployment. Code is available at https://github.com/yu-lin-li/ReBalance .

</details>


### [123] [AI Planning Framework for LLM-Based Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12710)
*Orit Shahnovsky,Rotem Dror*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将网页任务视为序贯决策过程的框架，通过将现代智能体架构映射到传统搜索算法（如BFS、DFS）来分析其行为，并引入新的评估指标和标注数据集以诊断系统失败原因。实验表明不同架构在成功率与技术性能上各有优劣。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在处理复杂网页任务时缺乏透明性，难以诊断失败原因和规划逻辑，因此需要一个可解释的框架来系统分析其行为。

Method: 将网页任务形式化为序贯决策问题，提出一种分类法，将Step-by-Step、Tree Search和Full-Plan-in-Advance智能体分别对应于BFS、Best-First Tree Search和DFS；设计五个新的轨迹评估指标，并构建包含794条人工标注轨迹的数据集进行验证。

Result: Step-by-Step智能体在整体任务成功率上更高（38%），更接近人类轨迹；而Full-Plan-in-Advance智能体在元素准确率等技术指标上表现更好（89%）。

Conclusion: 不同的智能体架构适用于不同应用场景，仅依赖成功率不足以评估性能，需结合本文提出的新指标进行综合判断。

Abstract: Developing autonomous agents for web-based tasks is a core challenge in AI. While Large Language Model (LLM) agents can interpret complex user requests, they often operate as black boxes, making it difficult to diagnose why they fail or how they plan. This paper addresses this gap by formally treating web tasks as sequential decision-making processes. We introduce a taxonomy that maps modern agent architectures to traditional planning paradigms: Step-by-Step agents to Breadth-First Search (BFS), Tree Search agents to Best-First Tree Search, and Full-Plan-in-Advance agents to Depth-First Search (DFS). This framework allows for a principled diagnosis of system failures like context drift and incoherent task decomposition. To evaluate these behaviors, we propose five novel evaluation metrics that assess trajectory quality beyond simple success rates. We support this analysis with a new dataset of 794 human-labeled trajectories from the WebArena benchmark. Finally, we validate our evaluation framework by comparing a baseline Step-by-Step agent against a novel Full-Plan-in-Advance implementation. Our results reveal that while the Step-by-Step agent aligns more closely with human gold trajectories (38% overall success), the Full-Plan-in-Advance agent excels in technical measures such as element accuracy (89%), demonstrating the necessity of our proposed metrics for selecting appropriate agent architectures based on specific application constraints.

</details>


### [124] [On Using Machine Learning to Early Detect Catastrophic Failures in Marine Diesel Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12733)
*Francesco Maione,Paolo Lino,Giuseppe Giannino,Guido Maione*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于随机森林和传感器数据偏差导数的新方法，用于早期检测船舶发动机的灾难性故障，能够在传统报警前预警，提升航行安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注部件的渐进退化，对突发性灾难故障关注不足，而此类故障突发且危害大，亟需有效的早期检测手段。

Method: 利用实际故障数据，通过随机森林模型预测发动机变量的实际读数与预期值之间偏差的导数，以捕捉异常动态的早期迹象。

Result: 该方法能在测量值达到临界阈值前检测到异常，早于工业中常用的报警机制，并通过仿真和真实数据验证了其有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效提前预警灾难性故障，为操作人员争取响应时间，避免损坏和意外动力丧失，具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: Catastrophic failures of marine engines imply severe loss of functionality and destroy or damage the systems irreversibly. Being sudden and often unpredictable events, they pose a severe threat to navigation, crew, and passengers. The abrupt nature makes early detection the only effective countermeasure. However, research has concentrated on modeling the gradual degradation of components, with limited attention to sudden and anomalous phenomena. This work proposes a new method for early detection of catastrophic failures. Based on real data from a failed engine, the approach evaluates the derivatives of the deviation between actual sensor readings and expected values of engine variables. Predictions are obtained by a Random Forest, which is the most suitable Machine Learning algorithm among the tested ones. Traditional methods focus on deviations of monitored signals, whereas the proposed approach employs the derivatives of the deviations to provide earlier indications of abnormal dynamics, and to alert that a rapid and dangerous event is breaking out within the system. The method allows the detection of anomalies before measurements reach critical thresholds and alarms are triggered, which is the common method in industry. Consequently, operators can be warned in advance and shut down the engine, then prevent damage and unexpected power loss. Moreover, they have the time to safely change the ship route and avoid potential obstacles. Simulation results conf irm the effectiveness of the proposed approach in anticipating occurrence of catastrophic failures. Validation on real-world data further reinforces the robustness and practical applicability of the method. It is worth noting that data acquisition to train the predictive algorithm is not a problem, since a Deep Learning-based data augmentation procedure is used.

</details>


### [125] [AI Model Modulation with Logits Redistribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12755)
*Zihan Wang,Zhongkui Ma,Xinguo Feng,Zhiyang Mei,Ethan Ma,Derui Wang,Minhui Xue,Guangdong Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为AIM的新模型调制范式，通过logits重分配策略实现单个模型的多样化行为，支持效用和焦点调制，无需重新训练且适用于多种任务和架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型模型通常需要针对不同需求进行适配，但维护多个专用版本效率低下。因此，需要一种能够使单一模型表现出多样化行为以满足终端需求的方法。

Method: 提出了AIM模型调制范式，引入logits重分配策略，支持效用调制和焦点调制，并基于联合概率分布的logits排序统计特性建立形式化理论基础，确保调控能力，整个过程无需依赖训练数据或重新训练。

Result: 在图像分类、语义分割和文本生成等任务上验证了AIM的有效性，涵盖ResNet、SegFormer和Llama等多种主流架构，展示了其在AI模型调制中的实用性与通用性。

Conclusion: AIM为大规模模型的灵活调控提供了一个高效、通用的解决方案，能够在不重新训练的情况下动态调整模型行为，满足多方需求，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Large-scale models are typically adapted to meet the diverse requirements of model owners and users. However, maintaining multiple specialized versions of the model is inefficient. In response, we propose AIM, a novel model modulation paradigm that enables a single model to exhibit diverse behaviors to meet the specific end requirements. AIM enables two key modulation modes: utility and focus modulations. The former provides model owners with dynamic control over output quality to deliver varying utility levels, and the latter offers users precise control to shift model's focused input features. AIM introduces a logits redistribution strategy that operates in a training data-agnostic and retraining-free manner. We establish a formal foundation to ensure AIM's regulation capability, based on the statistical properties of logits ordering via joint probability distributions. Our evaluation confirms AIM's practicality and versatility for Al model modulation, with tasks spanning image classification, semantic segmentation and text generation, and prevalent architectures including ResNet, SegFormer and Llama.

</details>


### [126] [ODRL Policy Comparison Through Normalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12926)
*Jaime Osvaldo Salas,Paolo Pareti,George Konstantinidis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种参数化规范化方法，将复杂的ODRL策略简化为仅包含权限的基本形式，并提供了保持语义的算法，以降低策略比较和处理的复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ODRL语言虽然成为数字权利政策表示的标准，但其复杂性阻碍了使用，且存在多种表达方式导致策略难以比较和处理。

Method: 基于最近定义的语义，提出参数化归一化方法，将ODRL策略分解为最小组件，将权限和禁止重写为仅含权限的形式，并简化逻辑约束。提供计算规范形式和简化数值及符号约束的算法。

Result: 证明了算法保持策略语义，结果大小复杂度在属性数量上呈指数级，在属性唯一值数量上线性增长；使复杂策略可在更基础的ODRL片段中表示，并将策略比较简化为规则一致性检查。

Conclusion: 该方法有效降低了ODRL策略的复杂性，提升了互操作性和可处理性，为策略比较和简化提供了理论支持和实用工具。

Abstract: The ODRL language has become the standard for representing policies and regulations for digital rights. However its complexity is a barrier to its usage, which has caused many related theoretical and practical works to focus on different, and not interoperable, fragments of ODRL. Moreover, semantically equivalent policies can be expressed in numerous different ways, which makes comparing them and processing them harder. Building on top of a recently defined semantics, we tackle these problems by proposing an approach that involves a parametrised normalisation of ODRL policies into its minimal components which reformulates policies with permissions and prohibitions into policies with permissions exclusively, and simplifies complex logic constraints into simple ones. We provide algorithms to compute a normal form for ODRL policies and simplifying numerical and symbolic constraints. We prove that these algorithms preserve the semantics of policies, and analyse the size complexity of the result, which is exponential on the number of attributes and linear on the number of unique values for these attributes. We show how this makes complex policies representable in more basic fragments of ODRL, and how it reduces the problem of policy comparison to the simpler problem of checking if two rules are identical.

</details>


### [127] [Efficient and Interpretable Multi-Agent LLM Routing via Ant Colony Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12933)
*Xudong Wang,Chaoning Zhang,Jiaquan Zhang,Chenghao Li,Qigan Sun,Sung-Ho Bae,Peng Wang,Ning Xie,Jie Zou,Yang Yang,Hengtao Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AMRO-S 是一种高效且可解释的多智能体系统路由框架，通过语义条件路径选择、任务特定信息素专家和质量门控异步更新机制，在降低推理成本与延迟的同时提升路由性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路由策略依赖昂贵的LLM选择器或静态策略，缺乏在动态负载和混合意图下的语义感知可控性，导致性能不稳定和资源利用低效。

Method: 将MAS路由建模为语义条件路径选择问题：1）使用监督微调的小型语言模型进行低开销意图推断；2）将路由记忆分解为任务特定的信息素专家以减少干扰；3）采用质量门控异步更新机制解耦推理与学习。

Result: 在五个公开基准和高并发压力测试中，AMRO-S 持续优于强基线方法，改善了质量-成本权衡，并通过结构化信息素模式提供可追溯的路由证据。

Conclusion: AMRO-S 实现了高效、透明且可控的多智能体路由，支持大规模部署中的稳定性能与良好资源利用率。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-driven Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) have demonstrated strong capability in complex reasoning and tool use, and heterogeneous agent pools further broaden the quality--cost trade-off space. Despite these advances, real-world deployment is often constrained by high inference cost, latency, and limited transparency, which hinders scalable and efficient routing. Existing routing strategies typically rely on expensive LLM-based selectors or static policies, and offer limited controllability for semantic-aware routing under dynamic loads and mixed intents, often resulting in unstable performance and inefficient resource utilization. To address these limitations, we propose AMRO-S, an efficient and interpretable routing framework for Multi-Agent Systems (MAS). AMRO-S models MAS routing as a semantic-conditioned path selection problem, enhancing routing performance through three key mechanisms: First, it leverages a supervised fine-tuned (SFT) small language model for intent inference, providing a low-overhead semantic interface for each query; second, it decomposes routing memory into task-specific pheromone specialists, reducing cross-task interference and optimizing path selection under mixed workloads; finally, it employs a quality-gated asynchronous update mechanism to decouple inference from learning, optimizing routing without increasing latency. Extensive experiments on five public benchmarks and high-concurrency stress tests demonstrate that AMRO-S consistently improves the quality--cost trade-off over strong routing baselines, while providing traceable routing evidence through structured pheromone patterns.

</details>


### [128] [Beyond Final Answers: CRYSTAL Benchmark for Transparent Multimodal Reasoning Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13099)
*Wayner Barrios,SouYoung Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CRYSTAL是一个包含6,372个实例的多模态推理诊断基准，通过可验证的中间步骤评估模型推理能力，并提出Match F1和Ordered Match F1两个新指标；研究发现现有MLLM普遍存在 cherry-picking 和推理顺序混乱等问题，并提出Causal Process Reward (CPR) 及其课程学习变体CPR-Curriculum，在无需人工标注步骤的情况下显著提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要依赖最终答案准确性评估模型推理能力，难以揭示模型在多步推理中的具体缺陷，如步骤遗漏、逻辑错序等；因此需要一个能通过可验证中间步骤诊断推理过程的精细基准。

Method: 构建CRYSTAL基准，包含6,372个需多步推理的实例；采用Delphi式流程由四个独立MLLM生成推理轨迹，经语义聚类与人工质检形成参考答案；提出Match F1和Ordered Match F1两种评估指标；进一步提出CPR（因果过程奖励）及其课程版本CPR-Curriculum，用于在训练中联合优化答案正确性与步骤对齐。

Result: 对20个MLLM的评估显示普遍存在的cherry-picking现象（精确率远高于召回率）、非单调缩放权衡以及推理链无序问题，没有模型能保持超过60%的匹配步骤在正确顺序中；CPR-Curriculum在GRPO训练框架下使Match F1提升+32%，而传统加性奖励策略失效。

Conclusion: 通过细粒度步骤评估可揭示传统准确率指标无法捕捉的系统性推理缺陷；引入的CPR和CPR-Curriculum为提升多步推理提供了有效且无需人工标注的新训练范式。

Abstract: We introduce **CRYSTAL** (*__C__lear __R__easoning via __Y__ielded __S__teps, __T__raceability and __L__ogic*), a diagnostic benchmark with 6,372 instances that evaluates multimodal reasoning through verifiable intermediate steps. We propose two complementary metrics: *Match F1*, which scores step-level precision and recall via semantic similarity matching, and *Ordered Match F1*, which further penalizes disordered reasoning chains. References are constructed through a Delphi-inspired pipeline where four independent MLLMs generate trajectories, aggregated via semantic clustering and validated through human quality gates. Evaluation of 20 MLLMs, including commercial frontier systems not used during benchmark construction, reveals systematic failures invisible to accuracy: universal cherry-picking (precision far exceeds recall), non-monotonic scaling trade-offs, and disordered reasoning where no competitive model preserves more than 60% of matched steps in correct order. Beyond evaluation, we propose the **Causal Process Reward (CPR)**, a multiplicative reward that couples answer correctness with step-level alignment, and **CPR-Curriculum**, which progressively increases reasoning difficulty during training. CPR-Curriculum achieves +32% Match F1 via GRPO where additive reward strategies fail, improving reasoning without manual step annotation.

</details>


### [129] [Steve-Evolving: Open-World Embodied Self-Evolution via Fine-Grained Diagnosis and Dual-Track Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13131)
*Zhengwei Xie,Zhisheng Chen,Ziyan Weng,Tingyu Wu,Chenglong Li,Vireo Zhang,Kun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Steve-Evolving是一个非参数化自演化框架，通过细粒度执行诊断与双轨知识蒸馏的闭环机制，提升开放世界具身智能体在长视野任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放世界的具身任务中，瓶颈不在于单步规划质量，而在于如何组织和演进交互经验。现有方法缺乏对执行过程的精细诊断与高效知识提炼机制。

Method: 提出Steve-Evolving框架，包含三个阶段：经验锚定、经验蒸馏和知识驱动的闭环控制。通过结构化经验元组和三层经验空间实现高效回忆；利用组合式诊断信号提取密集信息；将成功轨迹蒸馏为可复用技能，失败案例转化为可执行的防护规则；并将技能与规则注入LLM规划器，实现无需参数更新的持续演化。

Result: 在Minecraft MCU长视野任务套件上的实验表明，该方法相比静态检索基线 consistently improves performance。

Conclusion: Steve-Evolving通过闭环的知识演化机制，有效提升了具身智能体在复杂开放环境中的长期任务求解能力，验证了非参数化持续学习的可行性与优势。

Abstract: Open-world embodied agents must solve long-horizon tasks where the main bottleneck is not single-step planning quality but how interaction experience is organized and evolved. To this end, we present Steve-Evolving, a non-parametric self-evolving framework that tightly couples fine-grained execution diagnosis with dual-track knowledge distillation in a closed loop. The method follows three phases: Experience Anchoring, Experience Distillation, and Knowledge-Driven Closed-Loop Control. In detail, Experience Anchoring solidifies each subgoal attempt into a structured experience tuple with a fixed schema (pre-state, action, diagnosis-result, and post-state) and organizes it in a three-tier experience space with multi-dimensional indices (e.g., condition signatures, spatial hashing, and semantic tags) plus rolling summarization for efficient and auditable recall. To ensure sufficient information density for attribution, the execution layer provides compositional diagnosis signals beyond binary outcomes, including state-difference summaries, enumerated failure causes, continuous indicators, and stagnation/loop detection. Moreover, successful trajectories of Experience Distillation are generalized into reusable skills with explicit preconditions and verification criteria, while failures are distilled into executable guardrails that capture root causes and forbid risky operations at both subgoal and task granularities. Besides, Knowledge-Driven Closed-Loop Control retrieved skills and guardrails are injected into an LLM planner, and diagnosis-triggered local replanning updates the active constraints online, forming a continual evolution process without any model parameter updates. Experiments on the long-horizon suite of Minecraft MCU demonstrate consistent improvements over static-retrieval baselines.

</details>


### [130] [When Right Meets Wrong: Bilateral Context Conditioning with Reward-Confidence Correction for GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13134)
*Yu Li,Tian Lan,Zhengling Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种对比重构的GRPO方法，引入了双边上下文条件（BICC）和奖励-置信度校正（RCC），以增强推理模型训练效果，实验表明其在数学推理基准上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GRPO方法在优化时忽略同组内正确与错误解之间的结构对比信号，未能充分利用成功与失败推理轨迹间的比较信息。

Method: 提出BICC机制实现样本间直接信息流动，并通过RCC动态调整优势基线；基于GRPO目标隐式最大化正确与错误样本策略比率间隔的洞察进行设计。

Result: 在多个数学推理基准上验证了所提方法的有效性，显示对不同模型和算法均有稳定性能提升。

Conclusion: BICC和RCC能有效利用组内对比结构信号并稳定训练过程，无需额外采样或辅助模型，可广泛适用于各类GRPO变体。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has emerged as an effective method for training reasoning models. While it computes advantages based on group mean, GRPO treats each output as an independent sample during the optimization and overlooks a vital structural signal: the natural contrast between correct and incorrect solutions within the same group, thus ignoring the rich, comparative data that could be leveraged by explicitly pitting successful reasoning traces against failed ones. To capitalize on this, we present a contrastive reformulation of GRPO, showing that the GRPO objective implicitly maximizes the margin between the policy ratios of correct and incorrect samples. Building on this insight, we propose Bilateral Context Conditioning (BICC), a mechanism that allows the model to cross-reference successful and failed reasoning traces during the optimization, enabling a direct information flow across samples. We further introduce Reward-Confidence Correction (RCC) to stabilize training by dynamically adjusts the advantage baseline in GRPO using reward-confidence covariance derived from the first-order approximation of the variance-minimizing estimator. Both mechanisms require no additional sampling or auxiliary models and can be adapted to all GRPO variants. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements across comprehensive models and algorithms. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/Skylanding/BiCC}{https://github.com/Skylanding/BiCC}.

</details>


### [131] [Developing and evaluating a chatbot to support maternal health care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13168)
*Smriti Jha,Vidhi Jain,Jianyu Xu,Grace Liu,Sowmya Ramesh,Jitender Nagpal,Gretchen Chapman,Benjamin Bellows,Siddhartha Goyal,Aarti Singh,Bryan Wilder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于印度孕产妇健康的电话聊天机器人系统，结合了阶段感知分诊、混合检索和基于证据的LLM生成，并提出了适用于高风险、专家监督有限场景下的多方法评估流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源匮乏、健康素养低且医疗获取受限的地区，用户提出的孕产健康问题往往简短、模糊且夹杂多种语言，同时回答需要本地化背景支持，现有系统难以安全有效地应对这些问题。

Method: 系统采用三部分设计：(1) 阶段感知的分诊机制，将高风险请求路由至专家模板；(2) 对精选孕产/新生儿指南进行混合检索；(3) 基于大语言模型（LLM）的证据条件生成。评估方面构建了组件级与端到端测试流程，包括标注的分诊基准、合成的多证据检索基准、基于真实查询的LLM裁判比较以及专家验证。

Result: 分诊基准（N=150）实现86.7%的紧急情况召回率，并明确报告漏检与过度升级的权衡；合成检索基准（N=100）包含片段级证据标签；LLM-as-judge评估（N=781）使用临床医生设计的标准；并通过专家验证确认系统可靠性。

Conclusion: 在多语言、高噪声的医疗环境中，可信的医学助手需依赖纵深防御式设计与多方法评估体系，而非单一模型或评估手段。

Abstract: The ability to provide trustworthy maternal health information using phone-based chatbots can have a significant impact, particularly in low-resource settings where users have low health literacy and limited access to care. However, deploying such systems is technically challenging: user queries are short, underspecified, and code-mixed across languages, answers require regional context-specific grounding, and partial or missing symptom context makes safe routing decisions difficult.
  We present a chatbot for maternal health in India developed through a partnership between academic researchers, a health tech company, a public health nonprofit, and a hospital. The system combines (1) stage-aware triage, routing high-risk queries to expert templates, (2) hybrid retrieval over curated maternal/newborn guidelines, and (3) evidence-conditioned generation from an LLM. Our core contribution is an evaluation workflow for high-stakes deployment under limited expert supervision. Targeting both component-level and end-to-end testing, we introduce: (i) a labeled triage benchmark (N=150) achieving 86.7% emergency recall, explicitly reporting the missed-emergency vs. over-escalation trade-off; (ii) a synthetic multi-evidence retrieval benchmark (N=100) with chunk-level evidence labels; (iii) LLM-as-judge comparison on real queries (N=781) using clinician-codesigned criteria; and (iv) expert validation. Our findings show that trustworthy medical assistants in multilingual, noisy settings require defense-in-depth design paired with multi-method evaluation, rather than any single model and evaluation method choice.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [A Learning-Based Approach for Contact Detection, Localization, and Force Estimation of Continuum Manipulators With Integrated OFDR Optical Fiber](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12347)
*Mobina Tavangarifard,Jonathan S. Kacines,Qiyu Li,Farshid Alambeigi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于级联学习的框架（CLF），利用单根分布式OFDR光纤实现连续体机器人的接触检测、位置定位和力估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人在微创手术中应用广泛，但其分布变形使得力感知、接触检测、定位和力估计具有挑战性，尤其是在未知位置发生交互时。

Method: 采用梯度提升分类器进行接触检测，并使用CNN-FiLM模型预测沿机器人 backbone 的空间力分布，从而估计接触位置和力的大小。传感器为沿机器人一侧嵌入的单根OFDR光纤。

Result: 在受阻环境下的 tendon-driven 连续体机器人上实验验证，单根OFDR光纤可有效联合推断接触的发生、位置及力的大小。

Conclusion: 所提出的CLF框架结合单根分布式OFDR光纤，能够有效解决连续体机器人在复杂环境中多参数触觉感知的难题。

Abstract: Continuum manipulators (CMs) are widely used in minimally invasive procedures due to their compliant structure and ability to navigate deep and confined anatomical environments. However, their distributed deformation makes force sensing, contact detection, localization, and force estimation challenging, particularly when interactions occur at unknown arc-length locations along the robot. To address this problem, we propose a cascade learning-based framework (CLF) for CMs instrumented with a single distributed Optical Frequency Domain Reflectometry (OFDR) fiber embedded along one side of the robot. The OFDR sensor provides dense strain measurements along the manipulator backbone, capturing strain perturbations caused by external interactions. The proposed CLF first detects contact using a Gradient Boosting classifier and then estimates contact location and interaction force magnitude using a CNN--FiLM model that predicts a spatial force distribution along the manipulator. Experimental validation on a sensorized tendon-driven CM in an obstructed environment demonstrates that a single distributed OFDR fiber provides sufficient information to jointly infer contact occurrence, location, and force in continuum manipulators.

</details>


### [133] [Push, Press, Slide: Mode-Aware Planar Contact Manipulation via Reduced-Order Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12399)
*Melih Özcan,Ozgur S. Oguz,Umut Orguner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于接触拓扑选择和降阶运动学建模的模式感知框架，用于单臂和双臂平面操作，通过简化力学模型实现快速、无需优化的非抓取操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非抓取平面操作（如推、压滑）因接触力学复杂、欠驱动和摩擦不对称等问题难以控制，传统方法依赖计算昂贵的迭代控制，需要更高效的方法。

Method: 将复杂的力-扭极限面力学抽象为离散的直观模型库，系统地将不同接触拓扑映射到简化的非完整运动模型（如单车模型），并基于这些降阶模型生成轨迹，通过代数分配器分配可行且受摩擦约束的接触力，同时结合机械臂运动学保证长视野可行性。

Result: 在仿真中验证了该方法在多种单臂和双臂操作任务中的有效性，实现了快速、无需优化的控制。

Conclusion: 所提框架通过模式抽象和降阶建模，显著提升了非抓取平面操作的效率与可行性，适用于实时机器人控制。

Abstract: Non-prehensile planar manipulation, including pushing and press-and-slide, is critical for diverse robotic tasks, but notoriously challenging due to hybrid contact mechanics, under-actuation, and asymmetric friction limits that traditionally necessitate computationally expensive iterative control. In this paper, we propose a mode-aware framework for planar manipulation with one or two robotic arms based on contact topology selection and reduced-order kinematic modeling. Our core insight is that complex wrench-twist limit surface mechanics can be abstracted into a discrete library of physically intuitive models. We systematically map various single-arm and bimanual contact topologies to simple non-holonomic formulations, e.g. unicycle for simplified press-and-slide motion. By anchoring trajectory generation to these reduced-order models, our framework computes the required object wrench and distributes feasible, friction-bounded contact forces via a direct algebraic allocator. We incorporate manipulator kinematics to ensure long-horizon feasibility and demonstrate our fast, optimization-free approach in simulation across diverse single-arm and bimanual manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [134] [Beyond Motion Imitation: Is Human Motion Data Alone Sufficient to Explain Gait Control and Biomechanics?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12408)
*Xinyi Liu,Jangwhan Ahn,Edgar Lobaton,Jennie Si,He Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在基于强化学习的运动模仿学习框架中，加入足-地相互作用信息作为奖励项对人类步态运动学和动力学估计的影响，发现引入接触力和接触点信息可显著提升关节力矩预测的生物力学合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的运动模仿学习方法主要关注运动学匹配，可能忽视生物力学上的物理一致性，尤其是在关节动力学方面存在不足，因此需要探索如何通过引入动力学信息来改善模拟的真实性。

Method: 采用基于强化学习的模仿学习框架，比较仅使用运动学数据与额外加入足-地接触状态及接触力（如地面反作用力和压力中心）作为奖励项时，对人体步态动力学估计的效果。

Result: 结果表明，仅准确复现运动学并不能保证关节动力学的生物力学合理性；而加入足-地接触和接触力信息后，模拟出的关节力矩更接近逆动力学计算的真实值，显著提升了内外部动力学的真实性。

Conclusion: 在应用于人体生物力学和可穿戴机器人协同设计等领域的模仿学习中，必须引入基于动力学的奖励塑造，尤其是地面反作用力和压力中心信息，以实现物理上一致且更真实的步态表征。

Abstract: With the growing interest in motion imitation learning (IL) for human biomechanics and wearable robotics, this study investigates how additional foot-ground interaction measures, used as reward terms, affect human gait kinematics and kinetics estimation within a reinforcement learning-based IL framework. Results indicate that accurate reproduction of forward kinematics alone does not ensure biomechanically plausible joint kinetics. Adding foot-ground contacts and contact forces to the IL reward terms enables the prediction of joint moments in forward walking simulation, which are significantly closer to those computed by inverse dynamics. This finding highlights a fundamental limitation of motion-only IL approaches, which may prioritize kinematics matching over physical consistency. Incorporating kinetic constraints, particularly ground reaction force and center of pressure information, significantly enhances the realism of internal and external kinetics. These findings suggest that, when imitation learning is applied to human-related research domains such as biomechanics and wearable robot co-design, kinetics-based reward shaping is necessary to achieve physically consistent gait representations.

</details>


### [135] [Predictive and adaptive maps for long-term visual navigation in changing environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12460)
*Lucie Halodova,Eliska Dvorakova,Filip Majer,Tomas Vintr,Oscar Martinez Mozos,Feras Dayoub,Tomas Krajnik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文比较了不同地图管理技术在长期视觉导航中的表现，提出并评估了多种策略，实验表明，能够建模环境外观周期性变化并预测特征可见性的策略在机器人定位精度上优于不考虑时间演变的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现可靠且适应环境变化的长期导航，需要有效的地图管理技术来选择、更新和维护特征地图。

Method: 提出了多种地图管理策略，并在为期三个月的长期教学-重复导航实验中评估其对机器人定位精度的影响。

Result: 实验结果表明，建模环境外观周期性变化并预测特征可见性的策略在定位性能上优于不建模时间变化的策略。

Conclusion: 显式建模环境随时间变化的特征可见性有助于提升长期视觉导航系统的鲁棒性和准确性。

Abstract: In this paper, we compare different map management techniques for long-term visual navigation in changing environments. In this scenario, the navigation system needs to continuously update and refine its feature map in order to adapt to the environment appearance change. To achieve reliable long-term navigation, the map management techniques have to (i) select features useful for the current navigation task, (ii) remove features that are obsolete, (iii) and add new features from the current camera view to the map. We propose several map management strategies and evaluate their performance with regard to the robot localisation accuracy in long-term teach-and-repeat navigation. Our experiments, performed over three months, indicate that strategies which model cyclic changes of the environment appearance and predict which features are going to be visible at a particular time and location, outperform strategies which do not explicitly model the temporal evolution of the changes.

</details>


### [136] [One-Step Flow Policy: Self-Distillation for Fast Visuomotor Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12480)
*Shaolong Li,Lichao Sun,Yongchao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为One-Step Flow Policy (OFP)的自蒸馏框架，用于机器人控制中高效、高精度的单步动作生成，显著降低生成延迟并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成流和扩散模型虽能提供多模态动作分布，但其迭代采样导致推理延迟高，影响时间敏感任务中的控制频率和性能。

Method: 提出OFP框架，包含自一致性损失、自引导正则化和暖启动机制，实现无需预训练教师模型的单步高质量动作生成。

Result: 在56个模拟操作任务中，单步OFP优于100步扩散和流策略，生成速度提升超100倍；在RoboTwin 2.0的π₀.₅模型中也超越原10步策略。

Conclusion: OFP是一种实用且可扩展的解决方案，能够在保持高精度的同时实现低延迟的机器人控制。

Abstract: Generative flow and diffusion models provide the continuous, multimodal action distributions needed for high-precision robotic policies. However, their reliance on iterative sampling introduces severe inference latency, degrading control frequency and harming performance in time-sensitive manipulation. To address this problem, we propose the One-Step Flow Policy (OFP), a from-scratch self-distillation framework for high-fidelity, single-step action generation without a pre-trained teacher. OFP unifies a self-consistency loss to enforce coherent transport across time intervals, and a self-guided regularization to sharpen predictions toward high-density expert modes. In addition, a warm-start mechanism leverages temporal action correlations to minimize the generative transport distance. Evaluations across 56 diverse simulated manipulation tasks demonstrate that a one-step OFP achieves state-of-the-art results, outperforming 100-step diffusion and flow policies while accelerating action generation by over $100\times$. We further integrate OFP into the $π_{0.5}$ model on RoboTwin 2.0, where one-step OFP surpasses the original 10-step policy. These results establish OFP as a practical, scalable solution for highly accurate and low-latency robot control.

</details>


### [137] [COAD: Constant-Time Planning for Continuous Goal Manipulation with Compressed Library and Online Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12488)
*Adil Shiyas,Zhuoyun Zhong,Constantinos Chamzas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为COAD的框架，用于在连续目标参数化任务空间中实现常数时间运动规划。该方法通过将任务空间离散化为若干任务覆盖区域，并仅存储代表性根问题的解，利用轻量级适应模块快速生成目标路径，在保证高成功率的同时实现了微秒级查询和显著的库压缩效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对机器人操作中反复出现的运动规划问题，现有方法缺乏对连续任务空间的覆盖保证，且需存储大量解决方案，效率较低。因此需要一种既能保证覆盖性又能减少存储开销的高效规划方法。

Method: COAD框架将连续任务空间划分为有限个任务覆盖区域，仅离线求解代表性根问题并构建压缩解库；在线查询时通过常数时间检索根运动，并使用线性插值、动态运动基元或简单轨迹优化等轻量级模块进行快速适应以生成目标路径。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的多种机械臂和场景下验证了该框架的有效性，结果显示COAD在保持高成功率的同时实现了微秒级查询速度和显著的运动库压缩，且在效率和路径质量上优于基线方法。

Conclusion: COAD能够在连续目标参数化任务空间中实现高效、紧凑且具有覆盖保证的运动规划，适用于需要重复规划的机器人操作任务，为平衡规划速度与存储成本提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: In many robotic manipulation tasks, the robot repeatedly solves motion-planning problems that differ mainly in the location of the goal object and its associated obstacle, while the surrounding workspace remains fixed. Prior works have shown that leveraging experience and offline computation can accelerate repeated planning queries, but they lack guarantees of covering the continuous task space and require storing large libraries of solutions. In this work, we present COAD, a framework that provides constant-time planning over a continuous goal-parameterized task space. COAD discretizes the continuous task space into finitely many Task Coverage Regions. Instead of planning and storing solutions for every region offline, it constructs a compressed library by only solving representative root problems. Other problems are handled through fast adaptation from these root solutions. At query time, the system retrieves a root motion in constant time and adapts it to the desired goal using lightweight adaptation modules such as linear interpolation, Dynamic Movement Primitives, or simple trajectory optimization. We evaluate the framework on various manipulators and environments in simulation and the real world, showing that COAD achieves substantial compression of the motion library while maintaining high success rates and sub-millisecond-level queries, outperforming baseline methods in both efficiency and path quality. The source code is available at https://github.com/elpis-lab/CoAd.

</details>


### [138] [Robots that redesign themselves through kinematic self-destruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12505)
*Chen Yu,Sam Kriegman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种能够在生命周期内通过自我破坏主动参与自身设计的机器人，利用单一自回归序列模型和本体感受反馈动态优化其形态，实现更有效的运动控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人在部署前由外部过程预先设计，缺乏在实际环境中自主优化形态的能力。本文旨在探索机器人能否在运行过程中自主简化和优化其身体结构以提高运动性能。

Method: 机器人从随机组装的身体开始，仅依靠本体感受反馈，通过与地面剧烈摩擦使冗余部件在关节处断裂脱落，实现形态简化；使用在仿真中训练的通用自回归控制器决定何时及如何移除部件，并适应新的形态。

Result: 该策略在真实机器人上成功迁移并泛化到未见过的运动学结构，相比随机移除或不可变形态的策略，实现了更高效的前进运动。

Conclusion: 自我设计能力可能使机器人在某些场景下优于预设计机器人，而运动学上的自我破坏虽具不可逆性，却可作为一种普适的适应性策略。

Abstract: Every robot built to date was predesigned by an external process, prior to deployment. Here we show a robot that actively participates in its own design during its lifetime. Starting from a randomly assembled body, and using only proprioceptive feedback, the robot dynamically ``sculpts'' itself into a new design through kinematic self-destruction: identifying redundant links within its body that inhibit its locomotion, and then thrashing those links against the surface until they break at the joint and fall off the body. It does so using a single autoregressive sequence model, a universal controller that learns in simulation when and how to simplify a robot's body through self-destruction and then adaptively controls the reduced morphology. The optimized policy successfully transfers to reality and generalizes to previously unseen kinematic trees, generating forward locomotion that is more effective than otherwise equivalent policies that randomly remove links or cannot remove any. This suggests that self-designing robots may be more successful than predesigned robots in some cases, and that kinematic self-destruction, though reductive and irreversible, could provide a general adaptive strategy for a wide range of robots.

</details>


### [139] [Red-Teaming Vision-Language-Action Models via Quality Diversity Prompt Generation for Robust Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12510)
*Siddharth Srikanth,Freddie Liang,Sophie Hsu,Varun Bhatt,Shihan Zhao,Henry Chen,Bryon Tjanaka,Minjune Hwang,Akanksha Saran,Daniel Seita,Aaquib Tabrez,Stefanos Nikolaidis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Q-DIG的方法，通过结合质量多样性技术和视觉-语言模型，生成多样化的对抗性指令，以揭示视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的脆弱性，并提升其对不同语言描述的鲁棒性。实验表明，该方法在模拟和真实环境中均能有效提高任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在实际应用中对语言指令的表述非常敏感，难以预测其失败情况，因此需要一种方法来系统性地发现并改进这些模型在多样化指令下的脆弱性。

Method: 提出Q-DIG框架，将质量多样性（QD）技术与视觉-语言模型（VLMs）结合，大规模生成多样化且任务相关的自然语言指令，用于红队测试，识别导致VLA失败的输入。

Result: 在多个模拟基准上，Q-DIG相比基线方法发现了更多样化且有意义的失败模式；用户研究显示其生成的指令更自然、更接近人类表达；真实世界实验验证了其有效性，微调后的VLA在未见过的指令上成功率进一步提升。

Conclusion: Q-DIG是一种有前景的方法，可用于识别VLA系统的漏洞并增强其对语言变化的鲁棒性，从而推动通用机器人系统的发展。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have significant potential to enable general-purpose robotic systems for a range of vision-language tasks. However, the performance of VLA-based robots is highly sensitive to the precise wording of language instructions, and it remains difficult to predict when such robots will fail. To improve the robustness of VLAs to different wordings, we present Q-DIG (Quality Diversity for Diverse Instruction Generation), which performs red-teaming by scalably identifying diverse natural language task descriptions that induce failures while remaining task-relevant. Q-DIG integrates Quality Diversity (QD) techniques with Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to generate a broad spectrum of adversarial instructions that expose meaningful vulnerabilities in VLA behavior. Our results across multiple simulation benchmarks show that Q-DIG finds more diverse and meaningful failure modes compared to baseline methods, and that fine-tuning VLAs on the generated instructions improves task success rates. Furthermore, results from a user study highlight that Q-DIG generates prompts judged to be more natural and human-like than those from baselines. Finally, real-world evaluations of Q-DIG prompts show results consistent with simulation, and fine-tuning VLAs on the generated prompts further success rates on unseen instructions. Together, these findings suggest that Q-DIG is a promising approach for identifying vulnerabilities and improving the robustness of VLA-based robots. Our anonymous project website is at qdigvla.github.io.

</details>


### [140] [Beyond Dense Futures: World Models as Structured Planners for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12553)
*Minghao Jin,Mozheng Liao,Mingfei Han,Zhihui Li,Xiaojun Chang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出StructVLA，通过将生成式世界模型重构为显式的结构化规划器，利用稀疏且具有物理意义的结构帧进行视觉预知，从而提升机器人操作的可靠性和长视野任务的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于世界模型的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）方法在密集预测中存在冗余和误差累积问题，而稀疏方法缺乏明确的运动学对齐，导致规划与底层执行脱节。

Method: 提出StructVLA，利用内在运动线索（如夹爪状态变化和运动转折点）预测稀疏的结构化帧，并采用两阶段训练范式和统一离散token词汇表，将结构化预知映射到底层动作。

Result: 在SimplerEnv-WidowX上达到75.0%的成功率，在LIBERO上达到94.8%，并在真实场景中展现出对基础和复杂长视野任务的良好泛化能力。

Conclusion: StructVLA通过引入物理上有意义的结构化视觉预知，有效桥接了高层规划与低层控制，提升了机器人操作的可靠性与通用性。

Abstract: Recent world-model-based Vision-Language-Action (VLA) architectures have improved robotic manipulation through predictive visual foresight. However, dense future prediction introduces visual redundancy and accumulates errors, causing long-horizon plan drift. Meanwhile, recent sparse methods typically represent visual foresight using high-level semantic subtasks or implicit latent states. These representations often lack explicit kinematic grounding, weakening the alignment between planning and low-level execution. To address this, we propose StructVLA, which reformulates a generative world model into an explicit structured planner for reliable control. Instead of dense rollouts or semantic goals, StructVLA predicts sparse, physically meaningful structured frames. Derived from intrinsic kinematic cues (e.g., gripper transitions and kinematic turning points), these frames capture spatiotemporal milestones closely aligned with task progress. We implement this approach through a two-stage training paradigm with a unified discrete token vocabulary: the world model is first trained to predict structured frames and subsequently optimized to map the structured foresight into low-level actions. This approach provides clear physical guidance and bridges visual planning and motion control. In our experiments, StructVLA achieves strong average success rates of 75.0% on SimplerEnv-WidowX and 94.8% on LIBERO. Real-world deployments further demonstrate reliable task completion and robust generalization across both basic pick-and-place and complex long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [141] [From Woofs to Words: Towards Intelligent Robotic Guide Dogs with Verbal Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12574)
*Yohei Hayamizu,David DeFazio,Hrudayangam Mehta,Zainab Altaweel,Jacqueline Choe,Chao Lin,Jake Juettner,Furui Xiao,Jeremy Blackburn,Shiqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于导盲犬机器人的新型对话系统，利用大语言模型（LLM）实现导航计划和场景的语音化，以增强视障使用者与机器人之间的协作决策能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升导盲犬机器人在动态环境中的语言理解与空间感知能力，解决现有对话系统难以与移动机器人有效集成的问题。

Method: 开发了一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的对话系统，能够将导航计划和实时场景信息转化为自然语言输出，并通过人类实验和模拟实验评估不同的语言表达策略。

Result: 实验结果表明，该系统在导航任务中提高了沟通效率和路径规划的准确性，用户对不同语言表达策略表现出明确偏好。

Conclusion: 所提出的对话系统有效支持了操作员与机器人之间的语言交互，提升了导盲犬机器人的协作性与实用性。

Abstract: Assistive robotics is an important subarea of robotics that focuses on the well-being of people with disabilities. A robotic guide dog is an assistive quadruped robot that helps visually impaired people in obstacle avoidance and navigation. Enabling language capabilities for robotic guide dogs goes beyond naively adding an existing dialog system onto a mobile robot. The novel challenges include grounding language in the dynamically changing environment and improving spatial awareness for the human handler. To address those challenges, we develop a novel dialog system for robotic guide dogs that uses LLMs to verbalize both navigational plans and scenes. The goal is to enable verbal communication for collaborative decision-making within the handler-robot team. In experiments, we conducted a human study to evaluate different verbalization strategies and a simulation study to assess the efficiency and accuracy in navigation tasks.

</details>


### [142] [Skill-informed Data-driven Haptic Nudges for High-dimensional Human Motor Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12583)
*Ankur Kamboj,Rajiv Ranganathan,Xiaobo Tan,Vaibhav Srivastava*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种数据驱动的触觉轻推反馈框架，用于高维新运动技能学习，基于IOHMM建模和POMDP优化策略，实验表明该方法能显著加速学习并促进高效运动模式的形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升高维新运动任务中的学习效率，需要一种能够根据学习者动态调整的最优触觉反馈机制。

Method: 使用输入-输出隐马尔可夫模型（IOHMM）对人类运动学习的随机动力学进行建模，并将触觉轻推反馈设计问题建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），从而推导出最优反馈策略。

Result: 在30名受试者的实验中，采用POMDP策略的组别相比启发式反馈或无反馈组显著提升了任务表现，且更快速地形成了高效的低维运动协同模式。

Conclusion: 所提出的技能感知、数据驱动的POMDP框架能有效优化触觉轻推反馈，促进高维运动技能的高效学习。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a data-driven skill-informed framework to design optimal haptic nudge feedback for high-dimensional novel motor learning tasks. We first model the stochastic dynamics of human motor learning using an Input-Output Hidden Markov Model (IOHMM), which explicitly decouples latent skill evolution from observable kinematic emissions. Leveraging this predictive model, we formulate the haptic nudge feedback design problem as a Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP). This allows us to derive an optimal nudging policy that minimizes long-term performance cost, implicitly guiding the learner toward robust regions of the skill space. We validated our approach through a human-subject study ($N=30$) using a high-dimensional hand-exoskeleton task. Results demonstrate that participants trained with the POMDP-derived policy exhibited significantly accelerated task performance compared to groups receiving heuristic-based feedback or no feedback. Furthermore, synergy analysis revealed that the POMDP group discovered efficient low-dimensional motor representations more rapidly.

</details>


### [143] [CarPLAN: Context-Adaptive and Robust Planning with Dynamic Scene Awareness for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12607)
*Junyong Yun,Jungho Kim,ByungHyun Lee,Dongyoung Lee,Sehwan Choi,Seunghyeop Nam,Kichun Jo,Jun Won Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模仿学习的自动驾驶运动规划框架CarPLAN，通过增强驾驶上下文理解实现自适应决策。该方法结合位移感知预测编码（DPE）和上下文自适应多专家解码器（CMD），在nuPlan和Waymax基准上实现了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模仿学习方法在自动驾驶中主要依赖专家轨迹模仿，难以应对复杂多变的真实驾驶环境。为了提升安全性和鲁棒性，需要模型具备对驾驶上下文的理解能力和自适应决策能力。

Method: 提出CarPLAN框架，包含两个核心组件：1）位移感知预测编码（DPE），通过预测自动驾驶车辆与周围物体之间的未来位移向量来增强空间感知，并引入额外的损失项以考虑相对距离；2）上下文自适应多专家解码器（CMD），采用混合专家（MoE）架构，在每个Transformer层根据场景结构动态选择最优专家解码器，实现对多样化驾驶场景的自适应规划。

Result: 在nuPlan闭环仿真测试中，CarPLAN在所有指标上均达到最先进水平，尤其在Test14-Hard等复杂场景中表现出更强的鲁棒性；在Waymax基准上的实验也验证了其良好的跨平台泛化能力。

Conclusion: CarPLAN通过显式建模驾驶上下文和引入自适应机制，显著提升了模仿学习在自动驾驶运动规划中的性能和泛化能力，为复杂交通环境下的安全决策提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Imitation learning (IL) is widely used for motion planning in autonomous driving due to its data efficiency and access to real-world driving data. For safe and robust real-world driving, IL-based planning requires capturing the complex driving contexts inherent in real-world data and enabling context-adaptive decision-making, rather than relying solely on expert trajectory imitation. In this paper, we propose CarPLAN, a novel IL-based motion planning framework that explicitly enhances driving context understanding and enables adaptive planning across diverse traffic scenarios. Our contributions are twofold: We introduce Displacement-Aware Predictive Encoding (DPE) to improve the model's spatial awareness by predicting future displacement vectors between the Autonomous Vehicle (AV) and surrounding scene elements. This allows the planner to account for relational spacing when generating trajectories. In addition to the standard imitation loss, we incorporate an augmented loss term that captures displacement prediction errors, ensuring planning decisions consider relative distances from other agents. To improve the model's ability to handle diverse driving contexts, we propose Context-Adaptive Multi-Expert Decoder (CMD), which leverages the Mixture of Experts (MoE) framework. CMD dynamically selects the most suitable expert decoders based on scene structure at each Transformer layer, enabling adaptive and context-aware planning in dynamic environments. We evaluate CarPLAN on the nuPlan benchmark and demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across all closed-loop simulation metrics. In particular, CarPLAN exhibits robust performance on challenging scenarios such as Test14-Hard, validating its effectiveness in complex driving conditions. Additional experiments on the Waymax benchmark further demonstrate its generalization capability across different benchmark settings.

</details>


### [144] [Autonomous Integration and Improvement of Robotic Assembly using Skill Graph Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12649)
*Peiqi Yu,Philip Huang,Chaitanya Chawla,Guanya Shi,Jiaoyang Li,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于技能图（Skill Graph）表示的机器人装配系统自主集成与持续改进框架，通过语义级规划与执行接口的结合，实现了系统的快速集成、闭环性能优化和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人装配系统在集成新任务、适应新环境和持续性能提升方面依赖大量人工工程工作，缺乏自主性和可扩展性。

Method: 提出技能图框架，将机器人能力组织为基于动词的技能，关联语义描述（动词和名词）与可执行策略、前置条件、后置条件和评估器，支持语义级规划与控制器及感知模块的接口对接，并实现闭环数据收集与技能迭代优化。

Result: 验证了技能图能够统一系统配置、执行、评估与学习，支持快速系统集成和持续性能提升，提升系统的适应性与可重用性。

Conclusion: 该方法为构建自适应、可复用的机器人装配系统提供了可扩展的技术路径。

Abstract: Robotic assembly systems traditionally require substantial manual engineering effort to integrate new tasks, adapt to new environments, and improve performance over time. This paper presents a framework for autonomous integration and continuous improvement of robotic assembly systems based on Skill Graph representations. A Skill Graph organizes robot capabilities as verb-based skills, explicitly linking semantic descriptions (verbs and nouns) with executable policies, pre-conditions, post-conditions, and evaluators. We show how Skill Graphs enable rapid system integration by supporting semantic-level planning over skills, while simultaneously grounding execution through well-defined interfaces to robot controllers and perception modules. After initial deployment, the same Skill Graph structure supports systematic data collection and closed-loop performance improvement, enabling iterative refinement of skills and their composition. We demonstrate how this approach unifies system configuration, execution, evaluation, and learning within a single representation, providing a scalable pathway toward adaptive and reusable robotic assembly systems. The code is at https://github.com/intelligent-control-lab/AIDF.

</details>


### [145] [Learning Athletic Humanoid Tennis Skills from Imperfect Human Motion Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12686)
*Zhikai Zhang,Haofei Lu,Yunrui Lian,Ziqing Chen,Yun Liu,Chenghuai Lin,Han Xue,Zicheng Zeng,Zekun Qi,Shaolin Zheng,Qing Luan,Jingbo Wang,Junliang Xing,He Wang,Li Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为LATENT的系统，能够从不完美的人类运动数据中学习人形机器人打网球的技能，通过修正和组合原始技能片段，在仿真到现实的迁移中实现稳定的多回合对打。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于缺乏真实比赛中的完整人类网球动作数据，使人形机器人复现高动态的网球技能非常困难，因此需要一种能利用不完美数据进行学习的方法。

Method: LATENT利用仅包含网球基本动作片段的不完美人类运动数据，从中提取先验知识，通过动作修正与组合策略，在仿真环境中训练出具备自然运动风格的人形机器人击球策略，并设计了一系列增强仿真到现实迁移鲁棒性的方法。

Result: 该方法成功部署在Unitree G1人形机器人上，能够在多种条件下稳定回击高速来球并返回目标位置，实现在真实世界中与人类玩家持续进行多回合对打。

Conclusion: LATENT证明了即使使用不完整、不完美的人类运动数据，也能有效学习复杂、动态的人形机器人运动技能，为未来基于有限真实数据的机器人技能学习提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Human athletes demonstrate versatile and highly-dynamic tennis skills to successfully conduct competitive rallies with a high-speed tennis ball. However, reproducing such behaviors on humanoid robots is difficult, partially due to the lack of perfect humanoid action data or human kinematic motion data in tennis scenarios as reference. In this work, we propose LATENT, a system that Learns Athletic humanoid TEnnis skills from imperfect human motioN daTa. The imperfect human motion data consist only of motion fragments that capture the primitive skills used when playing tennis rather than precise and complete human-tennis motion sequences from real-world tennis matches, thereby significantly reducing the difficulty of data collection. Our key insight is that, despite being imperfect, such quasi-realistic data still provide priors about human primitive skills in tennis scenarios. With further correction and composition, we learn a humanoid policy that can consistently strike incoming balls under a wide range of conditions and return them to target locations, while preserving natural motion styles. We also propose a series of designs for robust sim-to-real transfer and deploy our policy on the Unitree G1 humanoid robot. Our method achieves surprising results in the real world and can stably sustain multi-shot rallies with human players. Project page: https://zzk273.github.io/LATENT/

</details>


### [146] [HaltNav: Reactive Visual Halting over Lightweight Topological Priors for Robust Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12696)
*Pingcong Li,Zihui Yu,Bichi Zhang,Sören Schwertfeger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为HaltNav的分层导航框架，结合轻量级文本地图osmAG与视觉-语言导航（VLN）技术，实现开放词汇、目标导向的自主导航。通过MLLM驱动的“大脑”模块进行高层任务解析与异常检测，并引入反应式视觉暂停（RVH）机制以应对环境变化，提升长距离导航的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN方法依赖密集指令或计算昂贵的地图，且在环境结构动态变化时易失败；本文旨在利用易获取的拓扑先验（osmAG）提升全局规划效率，同时增强对局部障碍的适应能力。

Method: 提出HaltNav框架：1）使用osmAG作为轻量拓扑先验进行全局路径规划；2）MLLM‘大脑’模块将全局路径分解为局部可执行子指令；3）引入反应式视觉暂停（RVH）机制检测局部异常（如关门），动态更新osmAG并触发重规划；4）设计基于生成模型的数据合成 pipeline 构建包含障碍物的硬负样本用于训练。

Result: 实验表明，HaltNav在无需繁琐语言指令的情况下优于多种基线方法，在环境变化下的长距离导航中展现出显著更高的成功率和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 结合轻量拓扑先验与MLLM推理的分层架构是实现高效、鲁棒开放词汇视觉语言导航的有效途径，尤其适用于现实世界中结构动态变化的场景。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) is shifting from rigid, step-by-step instruction following toward open-vocabulary, goal-oriented autonomy. Achieving this transition without exhaustive routing prompts requires agents to leverage structural priors. While prior work often assumes computationally heavy 2D/3D metric maps, we instead exploit a lightweight, text-based osmAG (OpenStreetMap Area Graph), a floorplan-level topological representation that is easy to obtain and maintain. However, global planning over a prior map alone is brittle in real-world deployments, where local connectivity can change (e.g., closed doors or crowded passages), leading to execution-time failures. To address this gap, we propose a hierarchical navigation framework HaltNav that couples the robust global planning of osmAG with the local exploration and instruction-grounding capability of VLN. Our approach features an MLLM-based brain module, which is capable of high-level task grounding and obstruction awareness. Conditioned on osmAG, the brain converts the global route into a sequence of localized execution snippets, providing the VLN executor with prior-grounded, goal-centric sub-instructions. Meanwhile, it detects local anomalies via a mechanism we term Reactive Visual Halting (RVH), which interrupts the local control loop, updates osmAG by invalidating the corresponding topology, and triggers replanning to orchestrate a viable detour. To train this halting capability efficiently, we introduce a data synthesis pipeline that leverages generative models to inject realistic obstacles into otherwise navigable scenes, substantially enriching hard negative samples. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our hierarchical framework outperforms several baseline methods without tedious language instructions, and significantly improves robustness for long-horizon vision-language navigation under environmental changes.

</details>


### [147] [Altered Thoughts, Altered Actions: Probing Chain-of-Thought Vulnerabilities in VLA Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12717)
*Tuan Duong Trinh,Naveed Akhtar,Basim Azam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型中链式思维（CoT）推理的中间文本计划对动作解码器的影响，发现动作解码器主要依赖于实体名称的完整性，而非推理质量或结构。实验表明，仅篡改推理轨迹中的物体名称即可显著降低任务成功率，而其他形式的文本破坏影响甚微。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨VLA模型中CoT推理生成的中间文本计划是否真正被动作解码器所利用，以及该内部文本通道是否存在潜在的安全漏洞。

Method: 设计了一个包含七种文本篡改方法的分类体系，分为三类攻击者（盲噪声、机械-语义、LLM自适应），并在40个LIBERO桌面操作任务上对最先进的推理型VLA模型进行测试。

Result: 物体名称替换使整体成功率下降8.3个百分点，在目标条件任务中达-19.3pp，个别任务中高达-45pp；而句子重排、空间方向反转、令牌噪声及70B参数LLM生成看似合理但错误的计划影响均小于±4pp。LLM攻击者因保持了实体接地结构而效果不佳。非推理VLA模型不受内部推理轨迹攻击影响，说明该漏洞专属于推理增强模型。

Conclusion: 动作解码器主要依赖推理轨迹中的实体引用完整性，而非推理逻辑或顺序结构，表明内部推理轨迹构成一个隐蔽且独特的安全威胁向量，现有输入验证防御无法检测此类攻击。

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models increasingly adopt chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning, generating a natural-language plan before decoding motor commands. This internal text channel between the reasoning module and the action decoder has received no adversarial scrutiny. We ask: which properties of this intermediate plan does the action decoder actually rely on, and can targeted corruption of the reasoning trace alone -- with all inputs left intact -- degrade a robot's physical task performance? We design a taxonomy of seven text corruptions organized into three attacker tiers (blind noise, mechanical-semantic, and LLM-adaptive) and apply them to a state-of-the-art reasoning VLA across 40 LIBERO tabletop manipulation tasks. Our results reveal a striking asymmetry: substituting object names in the reasoning trace reduces overall success rate by 8.3~percentage points (pp) -- reaching $-$19.3~pp on goal-conditioned tasks and $-$45~pp on individual tasks -- whereas sentence reordering, spatial-direction reversal, token noise, and even a 70B-parameter LLM crafting plausible-but-wrong plans all have negligible impact (within $\pm$4~pp). This asymmetry indicates that the action decoder depends on entity-reference integrity rather than reasoning quality or sequential structure. Notably, a sophisticated LLM-based attacker underperforms simple mechanical object-name substitution, because preserving plausibility inadvertently retains the entity-grounding structure the decoder needs. A cross-architecture control using a non-reasoning VLA confirms the vulnerability is exclusive to reasoning-augmented models, while instruction-level attacks degrade both architectures -- establishing that the internal reasoning trace is a distinct and stealthy threat vector invisible to input-validation defenses.

</details>


### [148] [Easy-IIL: Reducing Human Operational Burden in Interactive Imitation Learning via Assistant Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12769)
*Chengjie Zhang,Chao Tang,Wenlong Dong,Dehao Huang,Aoxiang Gu,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Easy-IIL是一种利用现成的基于模型的模仿方法作为辅助专家来减少人类在交互式模仿学习中操作负担的框架，仅需人类专家提供一次演示并干预关键状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少交互式模仿学习中的人类参与度，特别是在主动操作方面，同时保持学习性能。

Method: 提出Easy-IIL框架，使用离线模型为基础的模仿方法作为辅助专家，在大多数数据收集中替代人类操作，人类仅在任务接近失败的关键状态下进行干预。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界实验中，Easy-IIL显著减少了人类操作负担，并保持了与主流IIL基线相当的性能；用户研究表明其降低了人类专家的主观工作负荷。

Conclusion: Easy-IIL有效平衡了数据效率与人类努力，在减少人类操作投入的同时维持了良好的学习效果。

Abstract: Interactive Imitation Learning (IIL) typically relies on extensive human involvement for both offline demonstration and online interaction. Prior work primarily focuses on reducing human effort in passive monitoring rather than active operation. Interestingly, structured model-based imitation approaches achieve comparable performance with significantly fewer demonstrations than end-to-end imitation learning policies in the low-data regime. However, these methods are typically surpassed by end-to-end policies as the data increases. Leveraging this insight, we propose Easy-IIL, a framework that utilizes off-the-shelf model-based imitation methods as an assistant expert to replace active human operation for the majority of data collection. The human expert only provides a single demonstration to initialize the assistant expert and intervenes in critical states where the task is approaching failure. Furthermore, Easy-IIL can maintain IIL performance by preserving both offline and online data quality. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that Easy-IIL significantly reduces human operational burden while maintaining performance comparable to mainstream IIL baselines. User studies further confirm that Easy-IIL reduces subjective workload on the human expert. Project page: https://sites.google.com/view/easy-iil

</details>


### [149] [Motion-Specific Battery Health Assessment for Quadrotors Using High-Fidelity Battery Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12791)
*Joonhee Kim,Sanghyun Park,Donghyeong Kim,Eunseon Choi,Soohee Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的框架，用于评估四旋翼飞行器在不同飞行动作下的电池健康状况，发现即使平均能耗相同，不同的瞬态负载结构也会导致不同的电池退化路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数节能规划将电池视为简单的能量储存装置，忽略了飞行动作引起的动态电流负载对电池退化的加速作用，因此需要更精确的电池健康管理方法。

Method: 设计宽范围电流传感模块以捕获实际飞行中的电流特征，并基于退化耦合电化学模型校准高保真电池模型，通过模拟飞行负载分析不同飞行动作对电池退化模式的影响。

Result: 不同飞行运动会导致不同的电池退化模式，如锂库存损失和活性材料损失，且瞬态负载结构显著影响内部副反应。

Conclusion: 飞行运动显著影响电池退化，需采用兼顾能效与电池寿命的运动感知型电池管理策略。

Abstract: Quadrotor endurance is ultimately limited by battery behavior, yet most energy aware planning treats the battery as a simple energy reservoir and overlooks how flight motions induce dynamic current loads that accelerate battery degradation. This work presents an end to end framework for motion aware battery health assessment in quadrotors. We first design a wide range current sensing module to capture motion specific current profiles during real flights, preserving transient features. In parallel, a high fidelity battery model is calibrated using reference performance tests and a metaheuristic based on a degradation coupled electrochemical model.By simulating measured flight loads in the calibrated model, we systematically resolve how different flight motions translate into degradation modes loss of lithium inventory and loss of active material as well as internal side reactions. The results demonstrate that even when two flight profiles consume the same average energy, their transient load structures can drive different degradation pathways, emphasizing the need for motion-aware battery management that balances efficiency with battery degradation.

</details>


### [150] [FLUX: Accelerating Cross-Embodiment Generative Navigation Policies via Rectified Flow and Static-to-Dynamic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12806)
*Zeying Gong,Yangyi Zhong,Yiyi Ding,Tianshuai Hu,Guoyang Zhao,Lingdong Kong,Rong Li,Jiadi You,Junwei Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了DynBench，一个用于动态导航的基准测试平台，并结合静态协议实现六种基本导航任务的综合评估；同时提出FLUX，首个基于流的统一导航策略，通过线性化概率流显著提升推理效率，并在静态到动态的课程学习下实现最先进性能和零样本仿真到现实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主导航评估方法分散且缺乏统一框架，难以全面评估从静态目标到达至动态社交穿行等多种能力，因此需要一个统一的基准和通用导航策略。
